### `BUY-FV2-139` — Create the finite 3D MoolSocial brand-mark choreography

- Status: **R50 PROGRESSIVE BRAND LOCKUP FOUNDER APPROVED ON OPPO
  2026-08-01**
- Severity: **P1 global brand identity and motion consistency**
- Founder direction:
  - the current top M/wordmark treatment and its colour use do not feel
    consistently MoolSocial;
  - the animated identity should reveal `Mool`, introduce `Social`, then settle
    into one compact letterform such as `M` or a single combined `MS` mark; and
  - the reveal should use premium 3D motion on first open, app open and the
    first eligible brand interaction after meaningful inactivity.
- Isolation: this is not merged into `BUY-FV2-137`. It affects global brand
  presentation, launch/resume cadence and multiple app surfaces, and therefore
  requires its own founder visual comparison and additive brand baseline.
- Acceptance:
  1. Present founder-review variants for the final compact `M` and combined
     single-glyph `MS` outcomes before selecting the permanent mark.
  2. The finite sequence is `Mool` reveal, `Social` arrival, then 3D depth/
     rotation settle into the chosen compact mark; it never loops or autoplays
     continuously.
  3. Founder memory clarification (1 August 2026): identity artwork uses only
     navy plus the Indian-flag tricolour roles saffron, white and green. No
     fifth-colour, rainbow or unrelated gradient treatment may substitute for
     brand meaning.
  4. Automatic playback is bounded to eligible app/session entry. A replay
     after inactivity requires a deliberate first eligible brand interaction
     and a tested cooldown; ordinary scrolling/tapping never retriggers it.
  5. Reduced motion shows the chosen static compact mark immediately with zero
     3D rotation, depth travel or timed reveal.
  6. The animation is code-native Flutter, semantics expose one stable
     `MoolSocial` identity, hit ownership never moves, and launch/navigation
     readiness is not delayed.
  7. Android/iOS/compact/large-text captures, lifecycle/interruption tests,
     warm frame evidence, brand/reference gates and connected-OPPO replay pass
     before founder acceptance.
- Ordering: `BUY-FV2-139` begins only after the current catalogue-motion
  successor is decided. `BUY-FV2-138` route continuity remains a registered P1
  and is not silently cancelled by this brand ticket.

#### Founder rejection and R41 successor — 1 August 2026

The checksum-matched R41 FIX1 device candidate remains preserved as technically
qualified evidence but is visually rejected. The founder found no visible cold-
start animation, insufficient motion in the mark itself, an unsuitable M shape/
size and no useful MoolSocial meaning in either a lone `M` or combined `MS`.

The authorized successor keeps the existing per-surface logo-space dimensions
and replaces the rejected monogram outcome with the complete `MoolSocial`
wordmark. The finite code-native 3D treatment must emit the readable wordmark
from that stable owner without moving layout, moving the hit target, looping,
covering an interaction or delaying route readiness.

Successor acceptance:

1. A true process cold start paints an unmistakable held/start frame and then
   visibly animates the wordmark; autoplay cannot be consumed by a later or
   hidden brand owner before the launch owner paints.
2. `Mool` and `Social` receive coherent letter/word motion and bounded depth;
   no custom single `M`, `MS` glyph or unexplained compact letterform remains.
3. The final static state is the complete readable `MoolSocial` identity inside
   the unchanged logo owner on launch, Social and Buy.
4. Emission is restrained and clipped/contained so neighboring controls,
   Search, context text, content and hit ownership never move or become covered.
5. Only navy `#000080`, saffron `#FF9933`, white `#FFFFFF` and green `#138808`
   may paint the identity and its motion accents. Existing semantic colours
   retain their recorded business/accessibility meaning.
6. Motion is finite, event-driven and cooldown-bounded. Reduced motion renders

## Founder-approved deduplicated motion/theme tranche — 1 August 2026

The founder approved the read-only deduplicated ticket matrix for
implementation one logical ticket at a time. This approval reuses the owners
below and does not reopen accepted `BUY-FV2-136` tap acknowledgement or
`BUY-FV2-137` Search/category/vertical catalogue motion. It authorizes local
native Flutter implementation and qualification only; commit, push, deploy,
publish and merge remain prohibited.

Permanent tranche rules:

1. `BUY-FV2-139` is first and remains the sole global logo/wordmark owner.

   below. Their approved layout, copy, navigation and business meaning remain
   unchanged.

   error, warning, safety, medical, regulatory and accessibility meaning is not
   silently recoloured.

   resolves it honestly to zero/static state. No fake wait, progress, offer,
   entitlement, provider fact or perpetual decorative loop is allowed.
5. Runtime work is qualified one ticket at a time with compact/140-percent and
   Android/iOS-size evidence, exact profile APK/install/pull identity, physical
   OPPO replay, accessibility/lifecycle/performance evidence, two unchanged-
   source Buy regressions and all mandatory protected/reference/copy/brand/
   backend/data-egress/Social gates.

   FOUNDER VISUAL REVIEW PENDING`; morning visual review does not authorize a
   protected-baseline replacement before the founder accepts the exact object.
